\chapter{Introduction}\label{introduction}

This webbook contains all the code used for data analysis in study of
gut microbiomes of newts across ponds included in a restoration plan.

\section{Prepare the R environment}\label{prepare-the-r-environment}

\subsection{Environment}\label{environment}

To reproduce all the analyses locally, clone this repository in your
computer using:

\begin{verbatim}
RStudio > New Project > Version Control > Git
\end{verbatim}

And indicating the following git repository:

\begin{quote}
\url{https://github.com/alberdilab/vulture_metagenomics.git}
\end{quote}

Once the R project has been created, follow the instructions and code
chunks shown in this webbook.

\subsection{Libraries}\label{libraries}

The following R packages are required for the data analysis.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Base}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(R.utils)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(knitr)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(tidyverse)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(devtools)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(tinytable)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(rairtable)}

\CommentTok{\# For tree handling}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ape)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(phyloseq)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(phytools)}

\CommentTok{\# For plotting}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ggplot2)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ggrepel)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ggpubr)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ggnewscale)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(gridExtra)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ggtreeExtra)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ggtree)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ggh4x)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(pheatmap)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(stringr)}

\CommentTok{\# For statistics}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(spaa)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(vegan)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(Rtsne)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(geiger)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(hilldiv2)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(distillR)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ANCOMBC)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(lme4)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(FSA)}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(broom)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\chapter{Prepare data}\label{prepare-data}

\section{Load data}\label{load-data}

Load the original data files outputted by the bioinformatic pipeline.

\subsection{Sample metadata}\label{sample-metadata}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{sample\_metadata }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read\_tsv}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"data/DMB0117\_metadata.tsv.gz"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{rename}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{sample=}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{individuals }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{read.csv}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"vulture\_individuals.csv"}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\#Add State data}
\NormalTok{sample\_metadata}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{state }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{substr}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{word}\NormalTok{(sample\_metadata}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{region),}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}\FunctionTok{nchar}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{word}\NormalTok{(sample\_metadata}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{region))}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)}
\CommentTok{\#Join sample number with metadata}
\NormalTok{sample\_metadata }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(sample\_metadata, individuals, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{"sample"}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{removed\_samples }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ sample\_metadata[}\FunctionTok{which}\NormalTok{(sample\_metadata}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{metagenomic\_bases}\SpecialCharTok{==}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),]}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{sample}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Read counts}\label{read-counts}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{read\_counts }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read\_tsv}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"data/DMB0117\_counts.tsv.gz"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{rename}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{genome=}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{read\_counts }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ read\_counts[,}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\FunctionTok{which}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{match}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(read\_counts), removed\_samples)}\SpecialCharTok{!=}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)]}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Genome base hits}\label{genome-base-hits}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{genome\_coverage }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read\_tsv}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"data/DMB0117\_coverage.tsv.gz"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{rename}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{genome=}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{genome\_coverage }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ genome\_coverage[,}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\FunctionTok{which}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{match}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(genome\_coverage), removed\_samples)}\SpecialCharTok{!=}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)]}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Genome taxonomy}\label{genome-taxonomy}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{genome\_metadata }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read\_tsv}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"data/DMB0117\_mag\_info.tsv.gz"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{rename}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{length=}\NormalTok{mag\_size)}

\NormalTok{genome\_summary }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ genome\_metadata}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Genome tree}\label{genome-tree}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{genome\_tree }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read\_tree}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"data/DMB0117.tree"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{genome\_tree}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{tip.label }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{str\_replace\_all}\NormalTok{(genome\_tree}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{tip.label,}\StringTok{"\textquotesingle{}"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\#remove single quotes in MAG names}
\NormalTok{genome\_tree }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{keep.tip}\NormalTok{(genome\_tree, }\AttributeTok{tip=}\NormalTok{genome\_metadata}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{genome) }\CommentTok{\# keep only MAG tips}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Genome annotations}\label{genome-annotations}

Downloading individual annotation files from ERDA using information in
Airtable and writing them to a single compressed table takes a while.
The following chunk only needs to be run once, to generate the
\textbf{\emph{genome\_annotations}} table that is saved in the data
directory. Note that the airtable connection requires a personal access
token.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
 \FunctionTok{airtable}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"MAGs"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"appWbHBNLE6iAsMRV"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \CommentTok{\#get base ID from Airtable browser URL}
   \FunctionTok{read\_airtable}\NormalTok{(., }\AttributeTok{fields =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"ID"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"mag\_name"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"number\_genes"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"anno\_url"}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{id\_to\_col =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \CommentTok{\#get 3 columns from MAGs table}
   \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(mag\_name }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(genome\_metadata}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{genome,}\StringTok{".fa"}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \CommentTok{\#filter by MAG name}
   \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(number\_genes }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \CommentTok{\#genes need to exist}
   \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(anno\_url) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \CommentTok{\#list MAG annotation urls}
   \FunctionTok{pull}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
   \FunctionTok{read\_tsv}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \CommentTok{\#load all tables}
   \FunctionTok{rename}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{gene=}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{genome=}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{contig=}\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \CommentTok{\#rename first 3 columns}
   \FunctionTok{write\_tsv}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{file=}\StringTok{"data/genome\_annotations.tsv.xz"}\NormalTok{) }\CommentTok{\#write to overall compressed file}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{genome\_annotations }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read\_tsv}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"data/genome\_annotations.tsv.xz"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{rename}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{gene=}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{genome=}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{contig=}\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Create working objects}\label{create-working-objects}

Transform the original data files into working objects for downstream
analyses.

\subsection{Filter reads by coverage}\label{filter-reads-by-coverage}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{min\_coverage}\OtherTok{=}\FloatTok{0.3}
\NormalTok{read\_counts\_filt }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ genome\_coverage }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{across}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{where}\NormalTok{(is.numeric), }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(. }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}}\NormalTok{ min\_coverage, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{))) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{across}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ . }\SpecialCharTok{*}\NormalTok{ read\_counts[[}\FunctionTok{cur\_column}\NormalTok{()]])) }
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Transform reads into genome
counts}\label{transform-reads-into-genome-counts}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{readlength}\OtherTok{=}\DecValTok{150}
\NormalTok{genome\_counts }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ read\_counts }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{across}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{where}\NormalTok{(is.numeric), }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ . }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ (genome\_metadata}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{length }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ readlength) ))}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{readlength}\OtherTok{=}\DecValTok{150}
\NormalTok{genome\_counts\_filt }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ read\_counts\_filt }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{across}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{where}\NormalTok{(is.numeric), }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ . }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ (genome\_metadata}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{length }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ readlength) ))}
\NormalTok{genome\_counts\_filt }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{column\_to\_rownames}\NormalTok{(genome\_counts\_filt, }\StringTok{"genome"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{genome\_counts\_filt }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ genome\_counts\_filt[,}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{which}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{colSums}\NormalTok{(genome\_counts\_filt)}\SpecialCharTok{==}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{))]}
\NormalTok{genome\_counts\_filt }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{rownames\_to\_column}\NormalTok{(genome\_counts\_filt,}\StringTok{"genome"}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\#Remove samples with 0 counts of MAGs}
\NormalTok{sample\_metadata }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ sample\_metadata[}\FunctionTok{match}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(genome\_counts\_filt[}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]), sample\_metadata}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{sample),]}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{Distill annotations into
GIFTs}\label{distill-annotations-into-gifts}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{genome\_gifts }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{distill}\NormalTok{(genome\_annotations,GIFT\_db2,}\AttributeTok{genomecol=}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}\AttributeTok{annotcol=}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{9}\NormalTok{,}\DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{,}\DecValTok{19}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{verbosity=}\NormalTok{F)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]

\NormalTok{Identifiers in the annotation table: 1362 }
\NormalTok{Identifiers in the database: 1547 }
\NormalTok{Identifiers in both: 228 }
\NormalTok{Percentage of annotation table identifiers used for distillation: 16.74\%}
\NormalTok{Percentage of database identifiers used for distillation: 14.74\%}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Prepare color scheme}\label{prepare-color-scheme}

\href{www.alberdilab.dk}{AlberdiLab} projects use unified color schemes
developed for the \href{www.earthhologenome.org}{Earth Hologenome
Initiative}, to facilitate figure interpretation.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{colors }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{read\_tsv}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/earthhologenome/EHI\_taxonomy\_colour/main/ehi\_phylum\_colors.tsv"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{phylum\_colors }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ colors }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{right\_join}\NormalTok{(genome\_metadata, }\AttributeTok{by=}\FunctionTok{join\_by}\NormalTok{(phylum }\SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{ phylum)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{match}\NormalTok{(genome, genome\_tree}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{tip.label)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(phylum, colors) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
    \FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(phylum) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{pull}\NormalTok{(colors, }\AttributeTok{name=}\NormalTok{phylum)}
\CommentTok{\#Colors for samples}
\NormalTok{species\_colors }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#d83034"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#0d0d0d"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{sampletype\_colors }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#f45f74"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#008dff"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{sex\_colors }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#bdd373"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#003a7d"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{state\_colors }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#D55E00"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#CC79A7"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Wrap working objects}\label{wrap-working-objects}

All working objects are wrapped into a single Rdata object to facilitate
downstream usage.

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{save}\NormalTok{(sample\_metadata, }
\NormalTok{     genome\_metadata, }
\NormalTok{     read\_counts, }
\NormalTok{     genome\_counts, }
\NormalTok{     genome\_counts\_filt, }
\NormalTok{     genome\_tree,}
\NormalTok{     genome\_gifts, }
\NormalTok{     phylum\_colors,}
     \AttributeTok{file =} \StringTok{"data/data.Rdata"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\chapter{Data statistics}\label{data-statistics}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{load}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"data/data.Rdata"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Sequencing reads statistics}\label{sequencing-reads-statistics}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{sample\_metadata }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
    \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{Total=}\FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(reads\_post\_fastp }\SpecialCharTok{*} \DecValTok{150} \SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{1000000000}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{), }
              \AttributeTok{mean=}\FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(reads\_post\_fastp }\SpecialCharTok{*} \DecValTok{150} \SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{1000000000}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{),}
              \AttributeTok{sd=}\FunctionTok{sd}\NormalTok{(reads\_post\_fastp }\SpecialCharTok{*} \DecValTok{150} \SpecialCharTok{/} \DecValTok{1000000000}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{unite}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Average"}\NormalTok{,mean, sd, }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{" ± "}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{remove =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{tt}\NormalTok{()}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{DNA fractions}\label{dna-fractions}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{sequence\_fractions }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ read\_counts }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{pivot\_longer}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{genome, }\AttributeTok{names\_to =} \StringTok{"sample"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{values\_to =} \StringTok{"value"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

\NormalTok{seq\_stats}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{x.Coverage }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ seq\_stats}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{Total.Basepairs..MB. }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ seq\_stats}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{length.megabases.}

  \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =} \StringTok{"Coverage (binned)"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{y =} \StringTok{"Frequency of MAGs"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#Fraction of DNA that was recovered vs expected separated by species and sample type}

\NormalTok{singlem\_table }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sequence\_fractions }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{mags\_proportion =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{((mags\_bases }\SpecialCharTok{/}\NormalTok{ (mags\_bases }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ unmapped\_bases))}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{,}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{singlem\_proportion =} \FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(singlem\_fraction}\SpecialCharTok{*}\DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{,}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(sample,mags\_proportion,singlem\_proportion) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{mags\_proportion =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(singlem\_proportion }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, mags\_proportion)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \CommentTok{\#convert zeros to NA}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{singlem\_proportion =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(singlem\_proportion }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, singlem\_proportion)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \CommentTok{\#convert zeros to NA}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{singlem\_proportion =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(singlem\_proportion }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}}\NormalTok{ mags\_proportion, }\ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{, singlem\_proportion)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \CommentTok{\#if singlem is smaller, then NA, to simplify plot}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{singlem\_proportion =} \FunctionTok{ifelse}\NormalTok{(singlem\_proportion }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{, singlem\_proportion)) }\CommentTok{\#simplify}

\NormalTok{singlem\_table }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{pivot\_longer}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!}\NormalTok{sample, }\AttributeTok{names\_to =} \StringTok{"proportion"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{values\_to =} \StringTok{"value"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(sample\_metadata, }\AttributeTok{by =} \FunctionTok{join\_by}\NormalTok{(sample }\SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{ sample))  }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}

            \FunctionTok{geom\_line}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{group =}\NormalTok{ sample), }\AttributeTok{color =} \StringTok{"\#f8a538"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}

\section{Genome phylogeny}\label{genome-phylogeny}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Generate the phylum color heatmap}
\NormalTok{phylum\_heatmap }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read\_tsv}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"https://raw.githubusercontent.com/earthhologenome/EHI\_taxonomy\_colour/main/ehi\_phylum\_colors.tsv"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{right\_join}\NormalTok{(genome\_metadata, }\AttributeTok{by=}\FunctionTok{join\_by}\NormalTok{(phylum }\SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{ phylum)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{match}\NormalTok{(genome, genome\_tree}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{tip.label)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(genome,phylum) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{phylum =} \FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(phylum, }\AttributeTok{levels =} \FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(phylum))) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{column\_to\_rownames}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{var =} \StringTok{"genome"}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Generate  basal tree}
\NormalTok{circular\_tree }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{force.ultrametric}\NormalTok{(genome\_tree, }\AttributeTok{method=}\StringTok{"extend"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \CommentTok{\# extend to ultrametric for the sake of visualisation}
    \FunctionTok{ggtree}\NormalTok{(., }\AttributeTok{layout=}\StringTok{"fan"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{open.angle=}\DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{size=}\FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{***************************************************************}
\NormalTok{*                          Note:                              *}
\NormalTok{*    force.ultrametric does not include a formal method to    *}
\NormalTok{*    ultrametricize a tree \& should only be used to coerce    *}
\NormalTok{*   a phylogeny that fails is.ultrametric due to rounding {-}{-}  *}
\NormalTok{*    not as a substitute for formal rate{-}smoothing methods.   *}
\NormalTok{***************************************************************}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#Uncomment to include species names as tip labels as well as swapping geom\_tiplab() and geom\_tiplab2() comment below. Additionally this does not work with completeness or genome size rings}
\CommentTok{\#tree\_data \textless{}{-} circular\_tree$data \%\textgreater{}\%}
\CommentTok{\#  left\_join(genome\_metadata, by = c("label" = "genome"))  \# label = genome ID}
\CommentTok{\#circular\_tree$data \textless{}{-} tree\_data  \# update tree with species info}

\CommentTok{\# Add phylum ring}
\CommentTok{\#Code for using the species tip labels instead of MAG IDs}
\NormalTok{circular\_tree }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{gheatmap}\NormalTok{(circular\_tree, phylum\_heatmap, }\AttributeTok{offset=}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{width=}\FloatTok{0.1}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{colnames=}\ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
        \FunctionTok{scale\_fill\_manual}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{values=}\NormalTok{phylum\_colors) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
\CommentTok{\#        geom\_tiplab(aes(label = genome), size = 1.5, hjust = {-}.1) + }
        \FunctionTok{geom\_tiplab2}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{size=}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{hjust=}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\FloatTok{0.1}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
        \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{plot.margin =} \FunctionTok{margin}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{panel.margin =} \FunctionTok{margin}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{))}

\CommentTok{\# Flush color scale to enable a new color scheme in the next ring}
\NormalTok{circular\_tree }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ circular\_tree }\SpecialCharTok{+} \FunctionTok{new\_scale\_fill}\NormalTok{()}

\CommentTok{\# Add completeness ring}
\NormalTok{circular\_tree }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ circular\_tree }\SpecialCharTok{+}
        \FunctionTok{new\_scale\_fill}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
        \FunctionTok{scale\_fill\_gradient}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{low =} \StringTok{"\#d1f4ba"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{high =} \StringTok{"\#f4baba"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
        \FunctionTok{geom\_fruit}\NormalTok{(}
                \AttributeTok{data=}\NormalTok{genome\_metadata,}
                \AttributeTok{geom=}\NormalTok{geom\_bar,}
                \AttributeTok{mapping =} \FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x=}\NormalTok{completeness, }\AttributeTok{y=}\NormalTok{genome, }\AttributeTok{fill=}\NormalTok{contamination),}
                \AttributeTok{offset =} \FloatTok{0.55}\NormalTok{,}
                \AttributeTok{orientation=}\StringTok{"y"}\NormalTok{,}
              \AttributeTok{stat=}\StringTok{"identity"}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Add genome{-}size ring}
\NormalTok{circular\_tree }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{  circular\_tree }\SpecialCharTok{+}
        \FunctionTok{new\_scale\_fill}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
        \FunctionTok{scale\_fill\_manual}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{values =} \StringTok{"\#cccccc"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
        \FunctionTok{geom\_fruit}\NormalTok{(}
             \AttributeTok{data=}\NormalTok{genome\_metadata,}
             \AttributeTok{geom=}\NormalTok{geom\_bar,}
             \AttributeTok{mapping =} \FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x=}\NormalTok{length, }\AttributeTok{y=}\NormalTok{genome),}
                 \AttributeTok{offset =} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{,}
                 \AttributeTok{orientation=}\StringTok{"y"}\NormalTok{,}
         \AttributeTok{stat=}\StringTok{"identity"}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Add text}
\CommentTok{\#circular\_tree \textless{}{-}  circular\_tree +}
\CommentTok{\#        annotate(\textquotesingle{}text\textquotesingle{}, x=2.7, y=0, label=\textquotesingle{}            Phylum\textquotesingle{}, family=\textquotesingle{}arial\textquotesingle{}, size=3.5) +}
\CommentTok{\#        annotate(\textquotesingle{}text\textquotesingle{}, x=3.1, y=0, label=\textquotesingle{}                         Genome quality\textquotesingle{}, family=\textquotesingle{}arial\textquotesingle{}, size=3.5) +}
\CommentTok{\#        annotate(\textquotesingle{}text\textquotesingle{}, x=3.5, y=0, label=\textquotesingle{}                     Genome size\textquotesingle{}, family=\textquotesingle{}arial\textquotesingle{}, size=3.5)}

\CommentTok{\#Plot circular tree}
\NormalTok{circular\_tree }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{open\_tree}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{30}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{rotate\_tree}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{90}\NormalTok{)}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{family\_arrange }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ family\_summary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(relabun)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(family) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{mean=}\FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(relabun)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{arrange}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{mean) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(family) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{family=} \FunctionTok{sub}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\^{}f\_\_"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, family)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{pull}\NormalTok{()}

\NormalTok{family\_summary }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(genome\_metadata }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(family,phylum) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(),}\AttributeTok{by=}\FunctionTok{join\_by}\NormalTok{(family}\SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{family)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(sample\_metadata,}\AttributeTok{by=}\FunctionTok{join\_by}\NormalTok{(sample}\SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{sample)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{family=} \FunctionTok{sub}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\^{}f\_\_"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{, family)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(family }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ family\_arrange[}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{]) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{family=}\FunctionTok{factor}\NormalTok{(family,}\AttributeTok{levels=}\FunctionTok{rev}\NormalTok{(family\_arrange[}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{]))) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(relabun }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x=}\NormalTok{relabun, }\AttributeTok{y=}\NormalTok{family, }\AttributeTok{group=}\NormalTok{family, }\AttributeTok{color=}\NormalTok{phylum, }\AttributeTok{fill=}\NormalTok{phylum)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
        \FunctionTok{scale\_color\_manual}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{values=}\NormalTok{phylum\_colors[}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{8}\NormalTok{]) }\SpecialCharTok{+}

        \CommentTok{\#geom\_boxplot(alpha=0.2) +}

        \FunctionTok{facet\_nested}\NormalTok{(. }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ species }\SpecialCharTok{+}\NormalTok{ sample\_type)}\SpecialCharTok{+}
        \FunctionTok{theme\_minimal}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}

\NormalTok{genome\_counts\_test }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ genome\_counts\_test[, }\FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(metadata\_test)]}

\NormalTok{beta\_q1n }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ genome\_counts\_test }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{rowSums}\NormalTok{(. }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{select\_if}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}!}\FunctionTok{all}\NormalTok{(. }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{hillpair}\NormalTok{(., }\AttributeTok{q =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Run PCoA}

\CommentTok{\# Extract eigenvalues}
\NormalTok{eig\_vals }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ pcoa}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{eig}

\CommentTok{\# Convert to \% variation explained (only positive eigenvalues matter)}
\NormalTok{var\_explained }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ eig\_vals }\SpecialCharTok{/} \FunctionTok{sum}\NormalTok{(eig\_vals[eig\_vals }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{]) }\SpecialCharTok{*} \DecValTok{100}

\CommentTok{\# Extract coordinates}
\NormalTok{points }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.data.frame}\NormalTok{(pcoa}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{points)}
\NormalTok{points}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{sample }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(points)}

\CommentTok{\# Add group info}
\NormalTok{points }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{merge}\NormalTok{(points, metadata\_test, }\AttributeTok{by.x =} \StringTok{"sample"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{by.y =} \StringTok{"row.names"}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Plot with eigenvalues in axis labels}
\NormalTok{beta\_div }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(points, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ V1, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ V2, }\AttributeTok{color =}\NormalTok{ group, }\AttributeTok{shape =}\NormalTok{ sample\_type)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{geom\_point}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{stat\_ellipse}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{x =} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"PCoA 1 ("}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(var\_explained[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{], }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"\%)"}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{y =} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"PCoA 2 ("}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{round}\NormalTok{(var\_explained[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{], }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{"\%)"}\NormalTok{),}
    \AttributeTok{title =} \StringTok{"Beta Diversity"}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{theme\_minimal}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{+} 
  \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{panel.background =} \FunctionTok{element\_rect}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{fill =} \StringTok{"white"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{color =} \ConstantTok{NA}\NormalTok{),}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Example metadata and counts}

  \FunctionTok{column\_to\_rownames}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{var =} \StringTok{"genome"}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Fix sample matching}
\NormalTok{genome\_counts\_test }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ genome\_counts\_test[, }\FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(genome\_counts\_test) }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ metadata\_test}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{sample]}

\CommentTok{\# Beta diversity matrix}
\NormalTok{beta\_q1n }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ genome\_counts\_test }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{rowSums}\NormalTok{(. }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{select\_if}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}!}\FunctionTok{all}\NormalTok{(. }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{hillpair}\NormalTok{(., }\AttributeTok{q =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Metadata columns to test (must each have exactly 2 unique values)}
\NormalTok{grouping\_vars }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"species"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"sample\_type"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"state"}\NormalTok{)  }\CommentTok{\# adjust as needed}

\CommentTok{\# Different palettes for each variable}
\NormalTok{palette\_list }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{species =}\NormalTok{ species\_colors,}
  \AttributeTok{sample\_type   =}\NormalTok{ sampletype\_colors,}

\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Results containers}
\NormalTok{betadisper\_results }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{()}
\NormalTok{adonis\_results }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{()}

\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (var }\ControlFlowTok{in}\NormalTok{ grouping\_vars) \{}
  
  \CommentTok{\# "Other variables" are those NOT being compared}
\NormalTok{  other\_vars }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{setdiff}\NormalTok{(grouping\_vars, var)}
  
  \CommentTok{\# Loop through every unique combination of the other variables}
\NormalTok{  combos }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ metadata\_test }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{distinct}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{across}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{all\_of}\NormalTok{(other\_vars)))}
  
  \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (i }\ControlFlowTok{in} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(combos)) \{}
    \CommentTok{\# Subset metadata to this fixed combination}
\NormalTok{    subset\_meta }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ metadata\_test }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{across}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{all\_of}\NormalTok{(other\_vars), }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ .x }\SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{ combos[[}\FunctionTok{cur\_column}\NormalTok{()]][i]))}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Skip if not enough samples for comparison}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(subset\_meta[[var]])) }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{) }\ControlFlowTok{next}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Subset counts}
\NormalTok{    subset\_counts }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ genome\_counts\_test[, }\FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(genome\_counts\_test) }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ subset\_meta}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{sample]}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Recompute hillpair}
\NormalTok{    beta\_sub }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ subset\_counts }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{rowSums}\NormalTok{(. }\SpecialCharTok{!=} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{select\_if}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}!}\FunctionTok{all}\NormalTok{(. }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{hillpair}\NormalTok{(., }\AttributeTok{q =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)}
    
    \CommentTok{\# NMDS}
\NormalTok{    nmds }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{metaMDS}\NormalTok{(beta\_sub}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{S, }\AttributeTok{trymax =} \DecValTok{500}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{k =} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{trace =} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    scores\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{scores}\NormalTok{(nmds) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{as\_tibble}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{rownames =} \StringTok{"sample"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(subset\_meta, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{"sample"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(.data[[var]]) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{x\_cen =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(NMDS1, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{y\_cen =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(NMDS2, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{      ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{ungroup}\NormalTok{()}
    
\NormalTok{    combo\_label }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(other\_vars, }\StringTok{" = "}\NormalTok{, combos[i, ], }\AttributeTok{collapse =} \StringTok{", "}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    plot\_title  }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"NMDS of"}\NormalTok{, var, }\StringTok{"with"}\NormalTok{, combo\_label) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
      \FunctionTok{str\_wrap}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{width =} \DecValTok{45}\NormalTok{) }
    \CommentTok{\# Plot}
\NormalTok{    p }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(scores\_df, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ NMDS1, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ NMDS2, }\AttributeTok{color =}\NormalTok{ .data[[var]])) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{geom\_point}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{scale\_color\_manual}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{values =}\NormalTok{ palette\_list[[var]]) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{geom\_segment}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ x\_cen, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ y\_cen, }\AttributeTok{xend =}\NormalTok{ NMDS1, }\AttributeTok{yend =}\NormalTok{ NMDS2), }\AttributeTok{alpha =} \FloatTok{0.9}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{axis.title =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{12}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{face =} \StringTok{"bold"}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{axis.text =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{panel.background =} \FunctionTok{element\_blank}\NormalTok{(),}
        \AttributeTok{axis.line =} \FunctionTok{element\_line}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{size =} \FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{colour =} \StringTok{"black"}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{legend.text =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{legend.title =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{12}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{legend.position =} \StringTok{"right"}
\NormalTok{      ) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{title =}\NormalTok{ plot\_title}
\NormalTok{      )}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Save}
\NormalTok{   filename }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Figures/SubsettedData/NMDS\_"}\NormalTok{, var, }\StringTok{"\_Fix\_"}\NormalTok{, }
                   \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(combos[i, ], }\AttributeTok{collapse =} \StringTok{"\_"}\NormalTok{), }\StringTok{".png"}\NormalTok{)}
    \FunctionTok{ggsave}\NormalTok{(filename, }\AttributeTok{plot =}\NormalTok{ p, }\AttributeTok{width =} \DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{height =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{dpi =} \DecValTok{300}\NormalTok{)}
    
\CommentTok{\# Ensure rownames match and add \textquotesingle{}group\textquotesingle{}}
\NormalTok{subset\_meta2 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ subset\_meta }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{column\_to\_rownames}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"sample"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
\NormalTok{  .[}\FunctionTok{labels}\NormalTok{(beta\_sub}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{S), , drop }\OtherTok{=} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{] }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{group =}\NormalTok{ .data[[var]])}

\CommentTok{\# betadisper}
\NormalTok{bd }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{betadisper}\NormalTok{(beta\_sub}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{S, subset\_meta2}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{group)}
\NormalTok{bd\_res }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{permutest}\NormalTok{(bd, }\AttributeTok{pairwise =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Save betadisper results in a tidy tibble}

\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{  )}

\NormalTok{adonis\_results[[}\FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(var, i, }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{"\_"}\NormalTok{)]] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ad}

\subsection{Differential Abundance of Bacteria by Sample
Type}\label{differential-abundance-of-bacteria-by-sample-type}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{phylo\_metadata }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(sample\_metadata)}
\NormalTok{phylo\_metadata }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{column\_to\_rownames}\NormalTok{(phylo\_metadata, }\AttributeTok{var=}\StringTok{"sample"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{phylo\_metadata }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{sample\_data}\NormalTok{(phylo\_metadata)}

\NormalTok{phylo\_mag }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{otu\_table}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{column\_to\_rownames}\NormalTok{(genome\_counts\_filt, }\AttributeTok{var=}\StringTok{"genome"}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{taxa\_are\_rows=}\ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{otu\_mat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{otu\_table}\NormalTok{(phylo\_mag), }\StringTok{"matrix"}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Extract sample metadata as a dataframe}
\NormalTok{meta\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sample\_data}\NormalTok{(phylo\_metadata))}

\CommentTok{\# Make sure sample order matches}
\NormalTok{sample\_type\_vec }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ meta\_df[}\FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(otu\_mat), }\StringTok{"sample\_type"}\NormalTok{]}

\NormalTok{within\_group\_var }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(otu\_mat), }\ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(taxon) \{}
\NormalTok{  vals }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ otu\_mat[taxon, ]}
  \FunctionTok{tapply}\NormalTok{(vals, sample\_type\_vec)}
\NormalTok{\})}
\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(within\_group\_var) }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(otu\_mat)}

\CommentTok{\# Convert to matrix (taxa x groups)}
\NormalTok{var\_matrix }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{do.call}\NormalTok{(rbind, within\_group\_var)}

\CommentTok{\# Identify taxa with ALL zero or NA variance across groups}
\NormalTok{zero\_var\_taxa }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(var\_matrix)[}\FunctionTok{apply}\NormalTok{(var\_matrix, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(x) }\FunctionTok{all}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(x) }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ x }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{))]}

\CommentTok{\# Remove those taxa}
\NormalTok{otu\_mat\_filtered }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ otu\_mat[}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(otu\_mat) }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ zero\_var\_taxa, ]}
\NormalTok{phylo\_mag\_filtered }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{prune\_taxa}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{setdiff}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{taxa\_names}\NormalTok{(phylo\_mag), zero\_var\_taxa), phylo\_mag)}

\NormalTok{phylo\_mag\_filtered }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{otu\_table}\NormalTok{(otu\_mat\_filtered, }\AttributeTok{taxa\_are\_rows=}\ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{mag\_table }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{phyloseq}\NormalTok{(phylo\_mag\_filtered, phylo\_metadata)}

\NormalTok{res }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ancombc2}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ mag\_table,}
  \AttributeTok{fix\_formula =} \StringTok{"sample\_type"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{p\_adj\_method =} \StringTok{"holm"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{lib\_cut =} \DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{group =} \StringTok{"sample\_type"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{struc\_zero =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{neg\_lb =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{alpha =} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{global =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{verbose =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(lfc\_res)[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{] }\OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"Function"}

\NormalTok{lfc\_res }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(lfc\_res, }\AttributeTok{lfc =}\NormalTok{ res}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{res}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}lfc\_sample\_typeStomach contents\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{lfc\_res }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(lfc\_res, }\AttributeTok{p\_val =}\NormalTok{ res}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{res}\SpecialCharTok{$}\StringTok{\textquotesingle{}p\_sample\_typeStomach contents\textquotesingle{}}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# See significant taxa}

\NormalTok{test\_vars }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"species"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"sample\_type"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"state"}\NormalTok{)}
\CommentTok{\# Store results}
\NormalTok{ancom\_results }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{()}

\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (var }\ControlFlowTok{in}\NormalTok{ test\_vars) \{}
  \FunctionTok{message}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Running ANCOM{-}BC2 for: "}\NormalTok{, var)}

  \CommentTok{\# Convert phyloseq into matrix + metadata}
\NormalTok{  otu\_mat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{otu\_table}\NormalTok{(mag\_table), }\StringTok{"matrix"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  meta\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.data.frame}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sample\_data}\NormalTok{(mag\_table))}
  
  \CommentTok{\# Make sure sample order matches}
\NormalTok{  group\_vec }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ meta\_df[}\FunctionTok{colnames}\NormalTok{(otu\_mat), var]}
  
  \CommentTok{\# Compute within{-}group variance for each taxon}
\NormalTok{  within\_group\_var }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{lapply}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(otu\_mat), }\ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(taxon) \{}
\NormalTok{    vals }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ otu\_mat[taxon, ]}
    \FunctionTok{tapply}\NormalTok{(vals, group\_vec, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)   }\CommentTok{\# variance by group}
\NormalTok{  \})}
  \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(within\_group\_var) }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(otu\_mat)}
  
  \CommentTok{\# Convert to matrix (taxa × groups)}
\NormalTok{  var\_matrix }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{do.call}\NormalTok{(rbind, within\_group\_var)}
  
  \CommentTok{\# Identify taxa with ALL zero/NA variance across groups}
\NormalTok{  zero\_var\_taxa }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(var\_matrix)[}\FunctionTok{apply}\NormalTok{(var\_matrix, }\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(x) }\FunctionTok{all}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(x) }\SpecialCharTok{|}\NormalTok{ x }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{))]}
  
  \CommentTok{\# Remove zero{-}variance taxa}
\NormalTok{  otu\_mat\_filtered }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ otu\_mat[}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(otu\_mat) }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ zero\_var\_taxa, ]}
\NormalTok{  mag\_table\_filtered }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{prune\_taxa}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{setdiff}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{taxa\_names}\NormalTok{(mag\_table), zero\_var\_taxa), mag\_table)}
  
  
\NormalTok{  res }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ancombc2}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ mag\_table\_filtered,}
    \AttributeTok{fix\_formula =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(var),}
    \AttributeTok{p\_adj\_method =} \StringTok{"holm"}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{lib\_cut =} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{group =}\NormalTok{ var,}
    \AttributeTok{struc\_zero =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{neg\_lb =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{alpha =} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{global =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{verbose =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}
\NormalTok{  )}
  
  \CommentTok{\# Extract results for all taxa}
\NormalTok{  res\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ res}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{res }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{map}\NormalTok{(as.data.frame) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{bind\_cols}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{.id =} \StringTok{"metric"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{as\_tibble}\NormalTok{()}
  
  \CommentTok{\# Build tidy table}
\NormalTok{  lfc\_col }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{grep}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\^{}lfc\_"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(res}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{res), }\AttributeTok{value =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  p\_col   }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{grep}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\^{}p\_"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(res}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{res), }\AttributeTok{value =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{)}
  
\NormalTok{  tidy\_res }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{tibble}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{genome   =} \FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(res}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{res}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{taxon),}
    \AttributeTok{lfc      =}\NormalTok{ res}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{res[[lfc\_col]],}
    \AttributeTok{p\_val    =}\NormalTok{ res}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{res[[p\_col]],}
    \AttributeTok{test\_var =}\NormalTok{ var}
\NormalTok{  ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(lfc), }\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(p\_val)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{signif\_label =} \FunctionTok{cut}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      p\_val, }
      \AttributeTok{breaks =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\ConstantTok{Inf}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.001}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.01}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{, }\ConstantTok{Inf}\NormalTok{), }
      \AttributeTok{labels =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"***"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"**"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"*"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{""}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    ))}
  
  \CommentTok{\# Merge with genome metadata if available}
\NormalTok{  tidy\_res }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(tidy\_res, genome\_metadata, }\AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{"genome"}\NormalTok{)}
  
  \CommentTok{\# Store}
\NormalTok{  ancom\_results[[var]] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ tidy\_res}
\NormalTok{\}}

\CommentTok{\# Combine into one table}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{GIFTs\_test }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ GIFTs\_elements\_community\_no0}
\NormalTok{metadata\_test }\OtherTok{=}\NormalTok{ sample\_metadata}

\CommentTok{\# Metadata variables you want to test}
\NormalTok{vars }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"species"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"state"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"sample\_type"}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{palette\_list }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{species =}\NormalTok{ species\_colors,}
  \AttributeTok{sample\_type =}\NormalTok{ sampletype\_colors,}
  \AttributeTok{state =}\NormalTok{ state\_colors}
\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Empty lists to hold results}
\NormalTok{results\_adonis }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{()}
\NormalTok{results\_betadisper }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{()}

\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (var }\ControlFlowTok{in}\NormalTok{ vars) \{}
  
  \CommentTok{\# Other variables to fix}
\NormalTok{  other\_vars }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{setdiff}\NormalTok{(vars, var)}
  
  \CommentTok{\# All combinations of other vars}
\NormalTok{  combos }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(metadata\_test[other\_vars])}
  
  \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (i }\ControlFlowTok{in} \FunctionTok{seq\_len}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(combos))) \{}
\NormalTok{    combo }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ combos[i, , drop }\OtherTok{=} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{]}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Subset metadata based on fixed values}
\NormalTok{    subset\_meta }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ metadata\_test }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{across}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{all\_of}\NormalTok{(other\_vars), }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ .x }\SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{ combo[[}\FunctionTok{cur\_column}\NormalTok{()]]))}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Match GIFT samples to metadata}
\NormalTok{    subset\_gift }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ GIFTs\_test[}\FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(GIFTs\_test) }\SpecialCharTok{\%in\%}\NormalTok{ subset\_meta}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{sample, ]}
\NormalTok{    subset\_meta }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ subset\_meta[}\FunctionTok{match}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{rownames}\NormalTok{(subset\_gift), subset\_meta}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{sample), ]}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Skip if not enough samples}
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(subset\_meta) }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{4} \SpecialCharTok{||} \FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(subset\_meta[[var]])) }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{) }\ControlFlowTok{next}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Distance matrix}
\NormalTok{    dist\_func }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{vegdist}\NormalTok{(subset\_gift, }\AttributeTok{method =} \StringTok{"bray"}\NormalTok{)}
    
    \DocumentationTok{\#\# {-}{-}{-} Adonis2 {-}{-}{-}}
\NormalTok{    fmla }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.formula}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"dist\_func \textasciitilde{}"}\NormalTok{, var))}
\NormalTok{    ad }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{adonis2}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{      fmla,}
      \AttributeTok{data =}\NormalTok{ subset\_meta,}
      \AttributeTok{permutations =} \DecValTok{999}\NormalTok{,}
      \AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{"margin"}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{    ad\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ broom}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{tidy}\NormalTok{(ad) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{variable =}\NormalTok{ var,}
             \AttributeTok{fixed =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(combo), }\StringTok{"="}\NormalTok{, combo), }\AttributeTok{collapse =} \StringTok{", "}\NormalTok{))}
\NormalTok{    results\_adonis[[}\FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(var, i, }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{"\_"}\NormalTok{)]] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ ad\_df}
    
    \DocumentationTok{\#\# {-}{-}{-} Betadisper {-}{-}{-}}
\NormalTok{    bd }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{betadisper}\NormalTok{(dist\_func, subset\_meta[[var]])}
\NormalTok{    bd\_perm }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{permutest}\NormalTok{(bd)}
\NormalTok{    bd\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
      \AttributeTok{variable =}\NormalTok{ var,}
      \AttributeTok{fixed =} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(combo), }\StringTok{"="}\NormalTok{, combo), }\AttributeTok{collapse =} \StringTok{", "}\NormalTok{),}
      \AttributeTok{F\_value =}\NormalTok{ bd\_perm}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{tab[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"F"}\NormalTok{],}
      \AttributeTok{P\_value =}\NormalTok{ bd\_perm}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{tab[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Pr(\textgreater{}F)"}\NormalTok{]}
\NormalTok{    )}
\NormalTok{    results\_betadisper[[}\FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(var, i, }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{"\_"}\NormalTok{)]] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ bd\_df}
    
    \DocumentationTok{\#\# {-}{-}{-} NMDS Plot {-}{-}{-}}
    \FunctionTok{set.seed}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{123}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    nmds\_result }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{metaMDS}\NormalTok{(dist\_func, }\AttributeTok{k =} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{trymax =} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{    nmds\_scores }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.data.frame}\NormalTok{(nmds\_result}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{points)}
\NormalTok{    nmds\_scores[[var]] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ subset\_meta[[var]]}
    
    \CommentTok{\# Title with wrapping}
\NormalTok{    title\_text }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"GIFTs of "}\NormalTok{, var, }\StringTok{" with "}\NormalTok{, }
                         \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(combo), }\StringTok{"="}\NormalTok{, combo), }\AttributeTok{collapse =} \StringTok{", "}\NormalTok{))}
    
   \CommentTok{\# Pick correct palette}
\NormalTok{palette }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ palette\_list[[var]]}

\CommentTok{\# Compute centroids per group}
\NormalTok{centroids }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ nmds\_scores }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!!}\FunctionTok{sym}\NormalTok{(var)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x\_cen =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(MDS1, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
            \AttributeTok{y\_cen =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(MDS2, }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{))}

\CommentTok{\# Join back with scores}
\NormalTok{nmds\_scores }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ nmds\_scores }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
  \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(centroids, }\AttributeTok{by =}\NormalTok{ var)}

\NormalTok{p }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(nmds\_scores, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ MDS1, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ MDS2, }\AttributeTok{color =} \SpecialCharTok{!!}\FunctionTok{sym}\NormalTok{(var))) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{geom\_point}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{geom\_segment}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ x\_cen, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ y\_cen,}
                   \AttributeTok{xend =}\NormalTok{ MDS1, }\AttributeTok{yend =}\NormalTok{ MDS2),}
               \AttributeTok{alpha =} \FloatTok{0.6}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{scale\_color\_manual}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{values =}\NormalTok{ palette) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{panel.background =} \FunctionTok{element\_blank}\NormalTok{(),) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{title =} \FunctionTok{str\_wrap}\NormalTok{(title\_text, }\AttributeTok{width =} \DecValTok{60}\NormalTok{),}
       \AttributeTok{color =}\NormalTok{ var)}
    
\NormalTok{fixed\_text }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(combo, }\AttributeTok{collapse =} \StringTok{"\_"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{filename }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Figures/SubsettedData/GIFTs/"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"GIFTs\_"}\NormalTok{, var, }\StringTok{"\_"}\NormalTok{, fixed\_text, }\StringTok{".png"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{filename }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{gsub}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{" "}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\_"}\NormalTok{, filename)}

    \FunctionTok{ggsave}\NormalTok{(filename, }\AttributeTok{plot =}\NormalTok{ p, }\AttributeTok{width =} \DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{height =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{dpi =} \DecValTok{300}\NormalTok{)    }
\NormalTok{  \}}
\NormalTok{\}}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{element\_gift }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ GIFTs\_elements\_community\_no0 }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
  \FunctionTok{as.data.frame}\NormalTok{() }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
  \FunctionTok{rownames\_to\_column}\NormalTok{(., }\StringTok{"sample"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} 
  \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(sample\_metadata[}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"sample"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"sample\_type"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"species"}\NormalTok{,}\StringTok{"state"}\NormalTok{)], }\AttributeTok{by=}\StringTok{"sample"}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{meta\_vars }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"state"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"sample\_type"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"species"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{results\_list }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{()}

\NormalTok{palette\_list }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{state =}\NormalTok{ state\_colors ,}
  \AttributeTok{sample\_type =}\NormalTok{ sampletype\_colors,}
  \AttributeTok{species =}\NormalTok{ species\_colors}
\NormalTok{)}

\ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (var }\ControlFlowTok{in}\NormalTok{ meta\_vars) \{}
\NormalTok{  fixed\_vars }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{setdiff}\NormalTok{(meta\_vars, var)}
\NormalTok{  fixed\_combos }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ element\_gift }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{distinct}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{across}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{all\_of}\NormalTok{(fixed\_vars)))}
  
  \ControlFlowTok{for}\NormalTok{ (i }\ControlFlowTok{in} \FunctionTok{seq\_len}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(fixed\_combos))) \{}
\NormalTok{    combo }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ fixed\_combos[i, ]}
\NormalTok{    subset\_df }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ element\_gift }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{across}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{all\_of}\NormalTok{(fixed\_vars), }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}}\NormalTok{ .x }\SpecialCharTok{==}\NormalTok{ combo[[}\FunctionTok{cur\_column}\NormalTok{()]]))}
    
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(subset\_df[[var]])) }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{) }\ControlFlowTok{next}  \CommentTok{\# skip if not enough groups}
    
\NormalTok{    sig\_wilcox }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ subset\_df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{pivot\_longer}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(sample, }\FunctionTok{all\_of}\NormalTok{(meta\_vars)), }\AttributeTok{names\_to =} \StringTok{"trait"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{values\_to =} \StringTok{"value"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{group\_by}\NormalTok{(trait) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{summarise}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{p\_value =} \FunctionTok{tryCatch}\NormalTok{(}
          \FunctionTok{wilcox.test}\NormalTok{(value }\SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \FunctionTok{get}\NormalTok{(var))}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{p.value,}
          \AttributeTok{error =} \ControlFlowTok{function}\NormalTok{(e) }\ConstantTok{NA\_real\_}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \AttributeTok{mean\_1 =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(value[}\FunctionTok{get}\NormalTok{(var) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(subset\_df[[var]])[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]], }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{mean\_2 =} \FunctionTok{mean}\NormalTok{(value[}\FunctionTok{get}\NormalTok{(var) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(subset\_df[[var]])[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{]], }\AttributeTok{na.rm =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{mean\_difference =}\NormalTok{ mean\_1 }\SpecialCharTok{{-}}\NormalTok{ mean\_2,}
        \AttributeTok{direction =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{          mean\_difference }\SpecialCharTok{\textgreater{}} \DecValTok{0} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Higher in"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(subset\_df[[var]])[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]),}
\NormalTok{          mean\_difference }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \DecValTok{0} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Higher in"}\NormalTok{, }\FunctionTok{unique}\NormalTok{(subset\_df[[var]])[}\DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{]),}
          \ConstantTok{TRUE} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"No Difference"}
\NormalTok{        ),}
        \AttributeTok{.groups =} \StringTok{"drop"}
\NormalTok{      ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}
        \AttributeTok{p\_adjust =} \FunctionTok{p.adjust}\NormalTok{(p\_value, }\AttributeTok{method =} \StringTok{"BH"}\NormalTok{),}
        \AttributeTok{fixed\_species =}\NormalTok{ combo}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{species,}
        \AttributeTok{fixed\_state =}\NormalTok{ combo}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{state,}
        \AttributeTok{fixed\_sample\_type =}\NormalTok{ combo}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{sample\_type,}
        \AttributeTok{tested\_variable =}\NormalTok{ var}
\NormalTok{      ) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{filter}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!}\FunctionTok{is.na}\NormalTok{(p\_value))}
    
    \ControlFlowTok{if}\NormalTok{ (}\FunctionTok{nrow}\NormalTok{(sig\_wilcox) }\SpecialCharTok{==} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{) }\ControlFlowTok{next}
\NormalTok{    sig\_wilcox }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sig\_wilcox }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{p\_label =} \FunctionTok{case\_when}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{        p\_adjust }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \FloatTok{0.001} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"***"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{        p\_adjust }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \FloatTok{0.01}  \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"**"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{        p\_adjust }\SpecialCharTok{\textless{}} \FloatTok{0.05}  \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{"*"}\NormalTok{,}
        \ConstantTok{TRUE} \SpecialCharTok{\textasciitilde{}} \StringTok{""}
\NormalTok{      )) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{trait\_label =} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(trait, }\StringTok{" "}\NormalTok{, p\_label))}
    
\NormalTok{    sig\_wilcox }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sig\_wilcox }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
    \FunctionTok{mutate}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{negLogP =} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\FunctionTok{log10}\NormalTok{(p\_adjust))}

    
\NormalTok{    results\_list[[}\FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(var, combo}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{species, combo}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{state, combo}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{sample\_type, }\AttributeTok{sep =} \StringTok{"\_"}\NormalTok{)]] }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ sig\_wilcox}
    
    \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Create Heatmap {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\NormalTok{    heatmap\_data }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}}\NormalTok{ subset\_df }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(sample, }\FunctionTok{all\_of}\NormalTok{(var), }\FunctionTok{all\_of}\NormalTok{(fixed\_vars), }\FunctionTok{all\_of}\NormalTok{(sig\_wilcox}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{trait)) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{pivot\_longer}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{cols =}\NormalTok{ sig\_wilcox}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{trait, }\AttributeTok{names\_to =} \StringTok{"trait"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{values\_to =} \StringTok{"gift"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
      \FunctionTok{left\_join}\NormalTok{(sig\_wilcox }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%} \FunctionTok{select}\NormalTok{(trait, trait\_label, mean\_difference, direction, p\_adjust),}
                \AttributeTok{by =} \StringTok{"trait"}\NormalTok{)}
    
\NormalTok{    heatmap\_plot }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(heatmap\_data, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ sample, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ trait\_label, }\AttributeTok{fill =}\NormalTok{ gift)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{geom\_tile}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{color =} \StringTok{"white"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{linewidth =} \FloatTok{0.2}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{scale\_fill\_gradientn}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{colours =}\NormalTok{ palette\_list[[var]]) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{facet\_nested}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{vars}\NormalTok{(}\SpecialCharTok{!!}\FunctionTok{sym}\NormalTok{(var)), }\AttributeTok{scales =} \StringTok{"free"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{space =} \StringTok{"free"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{axis.text.x =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{angle =} \DecValTok{90}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{vjust =} \FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{hjust =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{),}
            \AttributeTok{strip.text.y =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{angle =} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{),}
            \AttributeTok{axis.text.y =} \FunctionTok{element\_text}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{angle =} \DecValTok{45}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{size =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{labs}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{y =} \StringTok{"Functional Attribute (with p{-}values)"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{x =} \StringTok{"Samples"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{fill =} \StringTok{"GIFT"}\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
      \FunctionTok{ggtitle}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Wilcoxon Test:"}\NormalTok{, var, }
                    \StringTok{"Fixed:"}\NormalTok{,}
                    \FunctionTok{paste}\NormalTok{(combo[fixed\_vars], }\AttributeTok{collapse =} \StringTok{", "}\NormalTok{)))}
    
    \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Save outputs {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\NormalTok{    out\_prefix }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Figures/SubsettedData/wilcox/"}\NormalTok{, var,}
                         \StringTok{"\_Fix\_"}\NormalTok{, combo}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{species,}
\NormalTok{                         combo}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{state,}\StringTok{"\_"}\NormalTok{,}
\NormalTok{                         combo}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{sample\_type)}
    
    \FunctionTok{ggsave}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{paste0}\NormalTok{(out\_prefix, }\StringTok{"\_heatmap.png"}\NormalTok{),}
           \AttributeTok{plot =}\NormalTok{ heatmap\_plot,}

    \CommentTok{\#Volcano Plot}
\NormalTok{    volcano\_plot }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggplot}\NormalTok{(sig\_wilcox, }\FunctionTok{aes}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{x =}\NormalTok{ mean\_difference, }\AttributeTok{y =}\NormalTok{ negLogP)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
